#### API-05 建档

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/records
Idempotency-Key: 7f3c9a1e-4b2d-11ef-9c8a-0242ac120002
```

```json
{
  "patient_id": 10021,
  "org_id": 1,
  "record_level": "STANDARD",
  "manage_doctor_id": 2001,
  "consult_doctor_id": 3001,
  "dept_id": 31,
  "remark": "屈光手术咨询转入建档"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 10086,
    "record_no": "RG00001234",
    "status": "ACTIVE",
    "opened_at": "2026-10-31T09:12:00",
    "next_action": "CREATE_BASELINE",
    "version": 0
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等：读 `Idempotency-Key`，命中 Redis `m11:idem:record:{key}` 直接返回首次结果。2) 调 U-01 取患者简要并写快照（`patient_name`/`gender`/`birth_date`/`age_at_first_visit`/`id_card_mask`/`phone_mask`/`empi_no`）；患者不存在报 `"1103002"`。3) 校验 `manage_doctor_id` 存在且具备屈光医师角色（U-06），否则 `"1103004"`。4) **一患一档**：插入依赖部分唯一索引 `uk_m11_refractive_record_patient_active`；冲突时捕获 `IntegrityError` 报 `"1103003"` 并在 `data` 返回已存在 `record_no`。5) 生成 `record_no`：Redis `INCR m11:code:RG:global` 补齐 8 位（`RG00001234`），DB 唯一索引兜底。6) 写 `m11_refractive_change_log`（`action = 'CREATE'`）与时间轴事件 `RECORD_CREATED`（`event_category = 'GOVERNANCE'`、`is_key_node = TRUE`）。7) 返回 `next_action = 'CREATE_BASELINE'` 引导前端跳 P04。 **错误码：** `"1103002"`、`"1103003"`、`"1103004"`。

#### API-06 维护档案基本信息

**请求**

```
PUT /api/v1/m11/records/RG00001234
```

```json
{
  "version": 12,
  "record_level": "KEY",
  "manage_doctor_id": 2001,
  "dept_id": 31,
  "tags": ["高度近视", "角膜偏薄", "干眼"],
  "remark": "角膜偏薄，重点关注"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "record_no": "RG00001234",
    "version": 13,
    "changed_fields": {
      "record_level": { "old": "STANDARD", "new": "KEY" },
      "remark": { "old": null, "new": "角膜偏薄，重点关注" }
    }
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 权限 `m11:record:update`；档案必须为 `ACTIVE`（已结档/已合并报 `"1103011"`）。2) 乐观锁 `version` 不匹配返回 `40900`（业务提示 `"1103005"`）。3) 生成字段级 diff，敏感字段判定用 `M11RefractiveChangeLog.detect_sensitive()`；写 `action = 'UPDATE'` 审计（含 `changed_fields` / `change_summary` / `field_count`）。4) **级别自动重算保护**：若档案已有当前生效基线，级别由系统按 3.3 规则自动判定；此时手工修改 `record_level` 会被系统值覆盖，并在响应中返回 `level_auto_corrected = true` 与原因（基线数据优先）。5) 主管医师变更需校验资质（`"1103004"`）。 **错误码：** `"1103001"`、`"1103004"`、`"1103005"`、`"1103011"`。

#### API-07 结档 / 重新激活

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/records/RG00001234/close
```

```json
{
  "version": 13,
  "action": "CLOSE",
  "close_reason": "患者已完成手术并长期随访结束"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "record_no": "RG00001234",
    "status": "CLOSED",
    "from_status": "ACTIVE",
    "closed_at": "2026-10-31T14:00:00",
    "version": 14
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 权限 `m11:record:update`。2) `action = 'CLOSE'`：`status` 必须为 `ACTIVE`；`close_reason` ≥ 5 字（否则 `"1103022"`）；置 `CLOSED` + `closed_at`，写时间轴事件 `RECORD_CLOSED`。3) `action = 'REOPEN'`：`status` 必须为 `CLOSED`；重新置 `ACTIVE` 并清空 `closed_at`/`close_reason`；若该患者已有其他在管档案则报 `"1103003"`。4) `MERGED` 档案不允许任何操作，报 `"1103011"`。5) 写审计日志（`action = 'CLOSE'` / `'REOPEN'`，`from_status` / `to_status`）。 **错误码：** `"1103003"`、`"1103005"`、`"1103011"`、`"1103022"`。

```json
      },
      {
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 权限 `m11:record:view`；档案不存在报 `"1103001"`。2) 按 `record_id` 取全部基线，`ORDER BY baseline_version DESC`（命中 `idx_m11_refractive_baseline_record`）。3) `include_void = false`（默认）时过滤 `baseline_status = 'VOID'`。4) `is_current` 由 `is_current = TRUE` 判定；同时返回 `current_baseline_no` 便于前端高亮。5) 眼别明细不在本接口返回（避免列表过重），需要明细时调 API-04。6) 响应说明多次手术场景：`baseline_version` 为 1、2 时对应「首刀前基线」与「再手术前基线」。 **错误码：** `"1103001"`。

```json
      "endothelial_density": 2710.00,
      "but_seconds": 5.2,
      "schirmer_mm": 9.0,
      "topo_pattern": "NORMAL"
    }
  ]
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "baseline_no": "JX202610310014",
    "baseline_version": 2,
    "baseline_status": "DRAFT",
    "is_current": false,
    "surgery_feasibility": "NOT_FEASIBLE",
    "eyes_feasibility": {
      "OD": "NOT_FEASIBLE", "OS": "NOT_FEASIBLE"
    },
    "warnings": [
      { "code": "THIN_CORNEA", "message": "右眼最薄点 478μm < 480μm，判不可行" },
      { "code": "DRY_EYE", "message": "BUT 4.5 秒 < 5 秒，判干眼" },
      { "code": "LENS_STOP_INSUFFICIENT", "message": "软镜停戴 11 天未达 14 天，基线暂不可确认" }
    ],
    "next_action": "CONFIRM"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等键 `m11:idem:record:{key}`。2) 档案必须 `ACTIVE`，否则 `"1103011"`。3) 若该档案存在 `exec_status = 'EXECUTING'` 的合并流程，报 `"1103037"`。4) 同基线内眼别不得重复（否则 `"1103010"`），最多 2 条。5) 临床数值校验：屈光度 0.25D 步进、柱镜/轴位互斥、角膜厚度 300~900μm、最薄点 ≤ 中央厚度、BCVA ≥ UCVA、K2 ≥ K1、眼轴 18~35mm —— 任一不符报 `"1103013"`。**单位约定：角膜厚度一律用 μm，`520μm` 不得换算成 mm 表达。** 6) `source_type = 'PREOP_EXAM'` 时 `exam_no` 必填，且同检查单已存在基线时报 `"1103012"`（依赖唯一索引 `uk_..._baseline_exam`）。7) `baseline_version = MAX(version) + 1`，并发冲突报 `"1103007"`。8) 计算等效球镜、平均曲率、角膜散光、散光类型（3.4.3）；计算 `effective_cct` 与双眼 `min_thinnest_cct`（3.4.1）；按最薄点与地形图给眼别可行性，再汇总为基线可行性。9) 停戴期校验（3.4.4）：从当前既往史取 `last_lens_off_date`，未达标时写入 `warnings` 但不阻断**保存草稿**。10) 写审计（`action = 'CREATE'`，`object_type = 'BASELINE'`）。 **错误码：** `"1103007"`、`"1103010"`、`"1103011"`、`"1103012"`、`"1103013"`、`"1103037"`。

```json
    { "eye_side": "OS", "cct": 520.00, "cct_thinnest": 496.00 }
  ]
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "baseline_no": "JX202610310014",
    "baseline_version": 2,
    "version": 1,
    "changed_fields": {
      "feasibility_note": { "old": null, "new": "右眼最薄点 478μm 低于 480μm 安全线，建议改 ICL 评估" }
    }
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 权限 `m11:record:update`；仅 `DRAFT` 基线可改，`CONFIRMED` 报 `"1103008"`，`VOID`/`SUPERSEDED` 报 `"1103011"`。2) 乐观锁 `version` 不匹配 `40900`（提示 `"1103005"`）。3) 全量校验同 API-09 第 5 条。4) 眼别明细按 `eye_side` 匹配更新，未传的眼别保持原值。5) 字段级 diff 写审计（`action = 'UPDATE'`，`object_type = 'BASELINE'`，`object_no = baseline_no`），敏感字段（`cct`/`cct_thinnest`/`sphere` 等）修改时置 `sensitive_field_hit = TRUE` 并要求 `reason`。 **错误码：** `"1103005"`、`"1103006"`、`"1103008"`、`"1103011"`、`"1103013"`。

#### API-11 确认基线

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/records/RG00001234/baselines/JX202610310014/confirm
```

```json
{
  "version": 1,
  "feasibility": "NOT_FEASIBLE",
  "feasibility_note": "右眼最薄点 478μm 低于 480μm 安全线，建议改 ICL 评估",
  "confirmed_by": 2001
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "baseline_no": "JX202610310014",
    "baseline_version": 2,
    "baseline_status": "CONFIRMED",
    "is_current": true,
    "superseded_baseline_no": "JX202403120003",
    "record_latest_baseline_id": 20031,
    "record_level_after": "KEY",
    "next_action": "MAKE_PLAN"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 权限 `m11:record:update` 且必须为屈光医师角色。2) **确认前置五项校验**：① 决策四项齐（屈光/曲率/厚度/眼轴），否则 `"1103014"`；② 可行性为 `CAUTION`/`NOT_FEASIBLE` 时 `feasibility_note` ≥ 10 字，否则 `"1103016"`；③ 停戴期达标（3.4.4），否则 `"1103017"`（可附「已书面确认已停戴」覆盖标记写入审计）；④ 屈光度来源可追溯：`source_optometry_no` 为空时给预警 `"1103015"`（不阻断，标记「未关联验光单」）；⑤ 档案 `ACTIVE`（`"1103011"`）。3) 事务内：旧 `is_current = TRUE` 的基线置 `SUPERSEDED`，本基线置 `CONFIRMED` + `is_current = TRUE`，依赖 `uk_m11_refractive_baseline_current` 保证唯一。4) 回填档案主表：`latest_baseline_id` / `latest_baseline_date` / `cct_od_min` / `cct_os_min` / `keratometry_max` / `corneal_astig_max` / `axial_length_max` / `endothelial_min` / `has_thin_cornea` / `has_irregular_topo` / `sphere_od_avg` 等冗余汇总；并按 3.3 规则重算 `record_level`。5) 写时间轴事件 `BASELINE_CONFIRMED`（`key_metrics` 含双眼关键值）。6) 写审计（`action = 'CONFIRM'`，`from_status = 'DRAFT'`，`to_status = 'CONFIRMED'`）。7) 删除 Redis 契约缓存 `m11:record:contract:{record_no}` 与 `m11:record:patient:{patient_id}`。 **错误码：** `"1103005"`、`"1103006"`、`"1103011"`、`"1103014"`、`"1103015"`、`"1103016"`、`"1103017"`。

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/records/RG00001234/baselines/JX202403120003/void
```

```json
{
  "version": 3,
  "void_reason": "术前检查做错患者（同名），需重新采集基线"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "baseline_no": "JX202403120003",
    "baseline_status": "VOID",
    "was_current": false,
    "record_current_baseline_no": "JX202610310014",
    "record_latest_baseline_id": 20031
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 权限 `m11:record:update`；基线不存在报 `"1103006"`。2) `void_reason` 必填且 ≥ 10 字，否则 `"1103018"`。3) **被方案引用保护**：若该基线被 `m11_surgery_plan.baseline_id` 引用（M11-04 只读校验），报 `"1103009"` 并返回引用方案号，要求先解绑方案。4) 若作废的是当前生效基线（`is_current = TRUE`），作废后需从剩余 `CONFIRMED` 基线中选版本最高者回填为 `is_current = TRUE`，并同步档案主表汇总；若无剩余已确认基线，则档案 `latest_baseline_id = NULL` 并提示「档案暂无生效基线」。5) 写审计（`action = 'DELETE'`，`reason` 必填）与时间轴事件（`event_category = 'GOVERNANCE'`）。 **错误码：** `"1103006"`、`"1103009"`、`"1103018"`。

#### API-17 术式可行性初判（试算）

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m11/records/RG00001234/baselines/feasibility
    ?cct_od=512&cct_thinnest_od=478&cct_os=520&cct_thinnest_os=496
    &topo_od=NORMAL&topo_os=NORMAL&has_keratoconus=false
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "effective_cct": {
      "OD": 478.00, "OS": 496.00, "min_thinnest": 478.00
    },
    "thresholds": {
      "thin_cornea_um": 480, "caution_cornea_um": 500
    },
    "eyes": [
      { "eye_side": "OD", "feasibility": "NOT_FEASIBLE", "reason": "最薄点 478μm < 480μm" },
      { "eye_side": "OS", "feasibility": "CAUTION", "reason": "最薄点 496μm 介于 480~500μm" }
    ],
    "baseline_feasibility": "NOT_FEASIBLE",
    "suggestion": [
      "角膜偏薄，建议评估 ICL/PRL 眼内植入方案（需核对前房深度与内皮）",
      "如坚持角膜激光，需主刀医师明确评估并记录风险告知"
    ],
    "equivalent_sphere": { "OD": -9.13, "OS": -8.50, "max_abs": 9.13 },
    "is_high_myopia": true
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 权限 `m11:record:view`；**纯计算接口不落库**，供 P04 步骤③④ 实时调用（防抖 500ms）。2) 保守取值（3.4.1）：`effective_cct = 最薄点 ?? 中央厚度`；`min_thinnest = MIN(OD, OS)`。3) 眼别判定：最薄点 < 480μm 或可疑圆锥角膜 → `NOT_FEASIBLE`；480~500μm → `CAUTION`；否则 `FEASIBLE`；缺值 → `NEED_MORE_EXAM`。4) 基线级判定取两眼最严重者（`NOT_FEASIBLE` > `CAUTION` > `NEED_MORE_EXAM` > `FEASIBLE`）。5) 等效球镜与高度近视判定（3.4.2）。6) `suggestion` 按判定结果生成（不可行时引导 ICL 评估，需谨慎时提示主刀评估并记录风险告知）。7) 入参中未传的数值回落到档案当前基线；均无值时按 `NEED_MORE_EXAM` 处理。8) 该接口结论**仅为基线层面初判**，不替代 M11-02 的准入判定与 M11-04 的切削计算。 **错误码：** `"1103001"`、`40001`。

```json
  "has_herpes_keratitis": false,
  "on_anticoagulant": false,
  "on_isotretinoin": false,
  "contact_lens_wear": true,
  "contact_lens_type": "SOFT",
  "last_lens_off_date": "2026-10-20",
  "contact_lens_years": 6.0,
  "dry_eye_diagnosed": true,
  "family_myopia": "YES",
  "family_keratoconus": "NO",
  "is_pregnant": false,
  "is_lactating": false,
  "smoking": "NEVER",
  "occupation_type": "OFFICE",
  "history_note": "长期软镜配戴，BUT 4.5 秒"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "history_version": 4,
    "is_current": true,
    "superseded_version": 3,
    "contraindication_hit": true,
    "contraindication_codes": ["DRY_EYE"],
    "risk_level": "HIGH",
    "risk_factors": ["DRY_EYE", "LONG_TERM_LENS_WEAR"],
    "lens_stop": {
      "type": "SOFT",
      "required_days": 14,
      "off_days": 11,
      "compliant": false
    },
    "record_risk_flags_updated": ["DRY_EYE", "THIN_CORNEA", "PRIOR_LASIK"]
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 权限 `m11:record:update`；幂等键。2) 档案必须 `ACTIVE`（`"1103011"`）。3) 字段必填判定：`on_anticoagulant = TRUE` 必须有明细（`"1103020"`）；`contact_lens_wear = TRUE` 必须有类型与末次摘镜日期（`"1103020"`）。4) 版本化：新版本 `history_version = MAX + 1`，旧当前版本置 `is_current = FALSE`，依赖 `uk_..._current` 保证唯一；若客户端提交时基于过期版本编辑（可选 `based_on_version` 参数与当前版本不符），报 `"1103036"`。5) 按 3.4.5 判定禁忌与风险：硬禁忌（圆锥角膜/孕期/哺乳）→ `contraindication_hit = TRUE` + 对应编码；干眼、长期镜片配戴、自身免疫、异维 A 酸等 → `risk_factors`；等级 `HIGH`/`MEDIUM`/`LOW`。6) 停戴期计算（3.4.4）：按类型取要求天数并计算 `off_days` 与 `compliant`。7) 更新档案主表 `risk_flags`（合并历史风险标记，不覆盖已消失项，保留档案级历史）。8) 写审计（`action = 'CREATE'`，`object_type = 'MEDICAL_HISTORY'`）与时间轴事件。 **错误码：** `"1103011"`、`"1103020"`、`"1103036"`。

#### API-19 档案时间轴

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m11/records/RG00001234/timeline
    ?key_only=false&event_category=CLINICAL&eye_side=OD
    &date_from=2024-01-01&date_to=2026-10-31&include_offline=true
    &cursor=eyJldmVudF9hdCI6IjIwMjYtMTAtMzBUMTY6MTA6MDAiLCJzZXEiOjEyfQ&limit=30
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,

    "items": [

        "key_metrics": {
          "sphere_od": -8.50, "sphere_os": -8.00,
          "cct_thinnest_od": 478.00, "cct_thinnest_os": 496.00,
          "axial_length_od": 26.18
        },
        "ref_module": "M11_03",
        "ref_no": "JX202610310014",
        "baseline_id": 20031,

        "recorded_offline": false
      },
      {

        "event_label": "术前检查完成",

        "event_at": "2026-10-31T09:05:00",
        "eye_side": "OU",
        "summary": "检查单 QJ202610310006 完成，准入结论 PASS",
        "key_metrics": { "admission_result": "PASS" },

  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"

```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 权限 `m11:record:view`；档案不存在报 `"1103001"`。2) **游标分页**（不用 `OFFSET`，避免长档案翻页性能衰减）：游标为 `(event_at, event_seq)` 的 base64 编码，`WHERE (event_at, event_seq) < (cursor_at, cursor_seq)`，命中 `idx_..._record`（含 `event_at DESC`）。3) `key_only = true` 时追加 `is_key_node = TRUE`（命中 `idx_..._key`）。4) `include_offline = false`（默认 true）时过滤 `recorded_offline = TRUE`。5) `event_category` / `eye_side` / 日期区间按需追加。6) `limit` 默认 30、上限 100。7) `total_estimate` 仅在首页返回（`COUNT(*)` 限分区裁剪范围内），后续页不返回。8) `OPTIMETRY_IMPORTED` 事件只返回 `optometry_no` 指针与「已更新档案验光指针」摘要，**不内联验光数值**（3.2.8 的架构红线）。9) 响应可缓存 Redis `m11:record:timeline:{record_no}:{cursor_hash}` TTL 60s。 **错误码：** `"1103001"`、`40001`。

#### API-21 创建打印 / 导出任务

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/records/export
Idempotency-Key: 2e4f6a1b-0c3d-11ef-9e5f-0242ac120002
```

```json
{
  "task_type": "RECORD_PDF",
  "record_no": "RG00001234",
  "scope_type": "SINGLE",
  "filter_json": null,
  "export_format": "PDF",
  "include_images": false,
  "template_code": "TPL_RECORD_V2",
  "approval_no": null
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "task_no": "DC202610310003",
    "task_type": "RECORD_PDF",
    "task_status": "PENDING",
    "progress_pct": 0,
    "record_count": 1,
    "expire_at": "2026-11-07T09:20:00",
    "watermark_text": "南石眼科 内部资料 仅限诊疗使用 李医师 2026-10-31"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `RECORD_PDF` / `RECORD_PRINT` 需 `m11:record:print`；`BATCH_EXPORT` 等导出需 `m11:record:export`，否则 `40300`。2) 参数校验：`SINGLE` 必须有 `record_no`（否则 `"1103025"`）；`MULTI`/`ALL` 必须有 `filter_json`（否则 `"1103025"`）；`ALL` 必须有 `approval_no`（否则 `"1103026"`）。3) 批量导出先估算档案数，超过 `M11.EXPORT.MAX_RECORDS`（5000）报 `"1103027"`。4) 关闭水印需 `m11:record:export`，否则 `"1103028"`。5) 生成 `task_no`（`DC` + 12 位）并落 `m11_refractive_export_task`（`PENDING`）；`expire_at = now + 7 天`；`watermark_text` 由服务端按「机构 + 用途 + 操作人 + 日期」生成（不接受客户端传入）。6) 投递 Celery 任务 `m11.record.export.render` 异步生成。7) 写审计（`action = 'EXPORT'` 或 `'PRINT'`，`object_type = 'EXPORT'`）。 **错误码：** `"1103025"`、`"1103026"`、`"1103027"`、`"1103028"`、`40300`。

#### API-23 导出任务状态与下载地址

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m11/records/export/DC202610310003?with_checksum=true
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "task_no": "DC202610310003",
    "task_type": "RECORD_PDF",
    "task_status": "DONE",
    "progress_pct": 100,
    "record_count": 1,
    "file_name": "屈光档案_RG00001234_20261031.pdf",
    "file_url": "/files/m11/export/DC202610310003.pdf?token=abc",
    "file_checksum": "9f2a1c8b7e6d5f4a3b2c1d0e9f8a7b6c5d4e3f2a1b0c9d8e7f6a5b4c3d2e1f0a",
    "checksum_verified": true,
    "file_size_bytes": 284512,
    "expire_at": "2026-11-07T09:20:00",
    "is_expired": false,
    "download_count": 1,
    "last_download_at": "2026-10-31T09:25:00",
    "error_message": null
  },

}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 权限 `m11:record:print`；任务不存在或不属于当前用户报 `"1103029"`。2) 任务进行中返回状态与进度，`file_url` 为 `null`。3) 已完成返回带签名 `file_url`，并把 `download_count + 1`、更新 `last_download_at`（写审计）。4) `with_checksum = true` 时后端重新计算对象存储文件 SHA-256 与 `file_checksum` 比对，返回 `checksum_verified`，用于举证文件未被篡改。5) 已过期（`expire_at < now`）时置 `is_expired = true`、`file_url = null`，下载请求报 `"1103033"`。6) 失败任务返回 `error_message`，并附 `can_retry` 标记（重试次数 < 5）。 **错误码：** `"1103029"`、`"1103033"`、`"1103034"`。

#### API-24 合并预检查

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/records/merge/precheck
```

```json
{
  "source_record_no": "RG00002345",
  "target_record_no": "RG00001234"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "source": {
      "record_no": "RG00002345",
      "patient_id": 10099,
      "patient_name": "张某某",
      "status": "ACTIVE",
      "baseline_count": 1,
      "eye_count": 2,
      "surgery_count": 1,
      "medical_history_count": 2,
      "timeline_event_count": 6
    },
    "target": {
      "record_no": "RG00001234",
      "patient_id": 10021,
      "patient_name": "张某某",
      "status": "ACTIVE",
      "baseline_count": 2,
      "eye_count": 4,
      "surgery_count": 2,
      "medical_history_count": 3,
      "timeline_event_count": 14
    },
    "conflicts": [
      {
        "conflict_type": "CURRENT_BASELINE",
        "severity": "WARN",
        "detail": "双方均有当前生效基线（源 v1 2024-03-12 / 目标 v2 2026-10-31）",
        "suggested_strategy": "KEEP_TARGET"
      },
      {
        "conflict_type": "CURRENT_MEDICAL_HISTORY",
        "severity": "WARN",
        "detail": "双方各有 1 条当前既往史，合并后保留目标版本",
        "suggested_strategy": "KEEP_TARGET"
      }
    ],
    "surgery_duplicates": [],
    "can_merge": true,
    "blockers": [],
    "estimated_moved": {
      "baseline": 1, "baseline_eye": 2, "surgery_history": 1,
      "medical_history": 2, "timeline_event": 6
    }
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 权限 `m11:record:merge_apply`。2) 校验两份档案存在且均 `ACTIVE`（否则 `"1103030"`）；源与目标不同（否则 `"1103030"`）。3) 统计双方数据量（各表 `COUNT`，5 个并发查询）。4) **冲突分析**：① 双方均有 `is_current = TRUE` 基线 → `CURRENT_BASELINE` 冲突；② 双方均有 `is_current = TRUE` 既往史 → `CURRENT_MEDICAL_HISTORY` 冲突；③ 手术史按 `eye_side + surgery_date + refractive_type` 判重 → `surgery_duplicates`；④ 时间轴按 `idempotency_key` 判重。5) `blockers`：任一档案处于 `EXECUTING` 合并流程、或档案已 `MERGED`（不可再作源）。6) 返回 `suggested_strategy`（默认 `KEEP_TARGET`，与业务惯例一致：保留数据更完整的档案）。7) **纯分析接口不落库**。 **错误码：** `"1103030"`、`"1103037"`。

#### API-25 提交合并申请

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/records/merge/apply
Idempotency-Key: 4a5b6c7d-8e9f-11ef-a0b1-0242ac120002
```

```json
{
  "source_record_no": "RG00002345",
  "target_record_no": "RG00001234",
  "apply_reason": "EMPI 判定为同一患者（M03-02 合并事件），需合并两份屈光档案",
  "conflict_strategy": "KEEP_TARGET"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "merge_no": "BH202610310002",
    "merge_type": "MERGE",
    "exec_status": "PENDING",
    "source_record_no": "RG00002345",
    "target_record_no": "RG00001234",
    "conflict_count": 2,
    "needs_audit": true,
    "audit_hint": "合并需双人复核：申请人不得审核本人提交的申请"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 权限 `m11:record:merge_apply`；幂等键。2) 内部调用 API-24 的预检查逻辑；存在 `blockers` 时拒绝（`"1103030"`）。3) `apply_reason` ≥ 10 字（`"1103030"`）；双方均有当前基线且未给 `conflict_strategy` 时报 `"1103030"`。4) 生成 `merge_no`（`BH` + 12 位），落 `m11_refractive_merge_log`：`merge_type = 'MERGE'`、`exec_status = 'PENDING'`、`apply_by = 当前用户`、`apply_reason`、`conflict_count`、`conflict_detail`（JSONB）。5) 写审计（`action = 'MERGE'`，`reason = apply_reason`）。6) 返回 `needs_audit = true`（合并必需双人复核，见 8.7.2）。 **错误码：** `"1103030"`、`"1103037"`。

#### API-26 审核合并 / 纠错申请

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/records/merge/BH202610310002/audit
```

```json
{
  "audit_result": "APPROVED",
  "audit_note": "两份档案确为同一患者，同意合并，保留目标档案基线"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "merge_no": "BH202610310002",
    "exec_status": "EXECUTING",
    "audit_result": "APPROVED",
    "audit_by_name": "王质控",
    "audit_at": "2026-10-31T15:00:00",
    "moved": {
      "baseline": 1, "baseline_eye": 2, "surgery_history": 1,
      "medical_history": 2, "timeline_event": 6
    },
    "source_record_status_after": "MERGED",
    "can_rollback_until": "2026-11-01T15:00:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 权限 `m11:record:audit`；记录不存在 `40400`；`exec_status ≠ 'PENDING'` 报 `"1103031"`。2) **不得自审**：`audit_by = apply_by` 时报 `"1103031"`（DB 约束 `ck_..._self_audit` 兜底）。3) `REJECTED` 时 `audit_note` ≥ 5 字（否则 `"1103031"`），状态置 `REJECTED`，不发任何数据变更。4) `APPROVED` 时：状态置 `EXECUTING`，然后在**单事务**内执行合并：① 从表 `record_id` 批量改为目标档案 ID（`baseline` / `baseline_eye` / `surgery_history` / `medical_history` / `timeline_event`）；② 处理基线冲突：按 `conflict_strategy`，源档案 `CONFIRMED` 基线置 `SUPERSEDED`、`is_current = FALSE`；③ 处理既往史冲突：源当前版本置 `is_current = FALSE`，版本号顺延到目标版本之后；④ 源档案置 `MERGED` + `merged_into_record_no` + `merged_at`；⑤ 重算目标档案冗余汇总。5) 事务提交后置 `DONE` + `exec_at`，写 `moved_*_count` 与时间轴事件 `RECORD_MERGED`；任一步失败则置 `FAILED` + `exec_error` 并整体回滚（报 `"1103032"`）。6) 返回 `can_rollback_until = exec_at + 24h`。7) 删除契约缓存。 **错误码：** `"1103031"`、`"1103032"`、`"1103037"`、`40400`。

#### API-27 变更审计查询

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m11/records/change-log?record_no=RG00001234&object_type=BASELINE
    &action=UPDATE&sensitive_only=true&date_from=2026-10-01&date_to=2026-10-31
    &page=1&page_size=20
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [
      {
        "log_no": "CL20261031000087",
        "record_no": "RG00001234",
        "object_type": "BASELINE",
        "object_no": "JX202610310014",
        "action": "UPDATE",
        "action_label": "修改",
        "changed_fields": {
          "cct_od": { "old": "520.00", "new": "512.00" },
          "cct_thinnest_od": { "old": "485.00", "new": "478.00" }
        },
        "change_summary": "右眼中央厚度 520μm → 512μm，最薄点 485μm → 478μm",
        "field_count": 2,
        "sensitive_field_hit": true,
        "reason": "复测地形图厚度，按新报告更正",
        "operator_id": 2001,
        "operator_name": "李医师",
        "operator_role": "DOCTOR",
        "client_type": "WEB",
        "created_at": "2026-10-31T11:22:00",
        "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
      }
    ],
    "total": 87,
    "page": 1,
    "page_size": 20,
    "pages": 5,
    "summary": {
      "sensitive_change_count": 12,
      "distinct_operators": 4
    }
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 权限 `m11:record:audit_view`。2) 按 `record_id`（`record_no` 反查）、`object_type`、`action`、`sensitive_field_hit`、日期区间过滤；日期区间必须给定或默认近 30 天（**用于分区裁剪**，命中对应月分区）。3) `LIMIT 20` 分页（`OFFSET` 在分区裁剪后可接受）；`total` 用窗口函数或独立 `COUNT`。4) `summary` 段统计区间内敏感字段变更数与涉及操作人数，用于质控看板。5) 只返回审计必要字段，不返回 `ip_address`（需 `m11:record:audit_detail` 权限单独查询）。 **错误码：** `"1103001"`、`40300`。

#### API-28 字段级纠错申请

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/records/correct
Idempotency-Key: 6b7c8d9e-0f1a-11ef-b2c3-0242ac120002
```

```json
{
  "record_no": "RG00001234",
  "target_table": "m11_refractive_baseline_eye",
  "target_row_id": 30021,
  "target_field": "cct_thinnest",
  "new_value": "478.00",
  "correct_reason": "地形图复测后最薄点厚度为 478μm，原录入 485μm 有误"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "merge_no": "BH202610310003",
    "merge_type": "CORRECT",
    "exec_status": "PENDING",
    "target_table": "m11_refractive_baseline_eye",
    "target_row_id": 30021,
    "target_field": "cct_thinnest",
    "old_value": "485.00",
    "new_value": "478.00",
    "impact_scope": "POSTOP_STAT_AFFECTED",
    "needs_audit": true,
    "affected": {
      "baseline_no": "JX202610310014",
      "referenced_by_plan": false,
      "feasibility_changed": true,
      "feasibility_after": "NOT_FEASIBLE"
    }
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 权限 `m11:record:merge_apply`；幂等键。2) `correct_reason` ≥ 10 字（`"1103030"`）。3) **旧值由服务端在同一事务内 `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` 读取**（不信任客户端传入），若新旧值相同报 `"1103038"`。4) 目标表白名单：`m11_refractive_record` / `m11_refractive_baseline` / `m11_refractive_baseline_eye` / `m11_refractive_surgery_history` / `m11_refractive_medical_history`；字段白名单另行校验（防止越权修改结构字段）。5) **影响面评估**：若目标字段属临床关键字段（厚度/屈光度/轴位/手术史），`impact_scope = 'POSTOP_STAT_AFFECTED'`；若该基线被方案引用则 `PLAN_AFFECTED`；并重算可行性判断是否变化。6) 生成 `merge_no`（`BH` + 12 位），落 `merge_log`（`merge_type = 'CORRECT'`，`old_value`/`new_value`/`correct_reason`/`impact_scope`），`exec_status = 'PENDING'` 等待 API-26 审核。7) 写审计（`action = 'CORRECT'`，`reason = correct_reason`）。 **错误码：** `"1103030"`、`"1103038"`、`40400`。

#### API-29 取屈光档案（P-02 内部契约）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m11/records/RG00001234
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "record_no": "RG00001234",
    "patient_id": 10021,
    "patient_name": "张某某",
    "status": "ACTIVE",
    "record_level": "KEY",
    "is_high_myopia": true,
    "is_prior_refractive_surgery": true,
    "prior_surgery_count": 1,
    "latest_admission_result": "PASS",
    "latest_baseline_date": "2026-10-31",
    "last_optometry_no": "YG202610300008",
    "baseline_stale": false,
    "baseline": {
      "baseline_no": "JX202610310014",
      "baseline_version": 2,
      "baseline_status": "CONFIRMED",
      "exam_date": "2026-10-31",
      "surgery_feasibility": "NOT_FEASIBLE",
      "thin_cornea_flag": true,
      "dry_eye_flag": true,
      "eyes": [
        { "eye_side": "OD", "sphere": -8.50, "cylinder": -1.25, "axis": 180,
          "cct": 512.00, "cct_thinnest": 478.00, "axial_length": 26.18,
          "acd": 3.20, "endothelial_density": 2650.00, "eye_feasibility": "NOT_FEASIBLE" },
        { "eye_side": "OS", "sphere": -8.00, "cylinder": -1.00, "axis": 10,
          "cct": 520.00, "cct_thinnest": 496.00, "axial_length": 25.90,
          "acd": 3.25, "endothelial_density": 2710.00, "eye_feasibility": "NOT_FEASIBLE" }
      ]
    },
    "surgery_histories": [
      { "surgery_seq": 1, "source_type": "OTHER_HOSPITAL", "surgery_date": "2019-06-12",
        "refractive_type": "FS_LASIK", "eye_side": "OU", "outcome_result": "PARTIAL",
        "verified_flag": true }
    ],
    "contraindication_codes": ["DRY_EYE", "PRIOR_LASIK"],
    "state_version": 12,
    "updated_at": "2026-10-31T09:12:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) **内部服务鉴权**：校验 `X-Service-Token` 或 mTLS，不接受用户 token；权限列记为「内部服务」。2) 查询命中 `uk_m11_refractive_record_no` 单表取主表，再用 `uk_m11_refractive_baseline_current` 取当前基线、`idx_m11_refractive_baseline_eye_baseline` 取双眼、`idx_..._surgery_history_record` 取手术史 —— **4 条 SQL 完成，禁止 N+1**，保证 P95 ≤ 100ms。3) 档案不存在/已删除报 `"1103001"`；已结档档案正常返回（`status = 'CLOSED'`）；已合并档案返回 `status = 'MERGED'` 与 `merged_into_record_no`（由调用方决定是否跟随）。4) `baseline_stale` 按 3.4.6 计算（> 365 天为 `true`），供 M07/M22 判断数据新鲜度。5) `last_optometry_no` 仅返回指针，**契约不返回任何验光数值**（除基线快照内已带 `source_optometry_no` 溯源的值）。6) `state_version` 为乐观锁版本，供消费方做条件缓存。7) 缓存：Redis `m11:record:contract:{record_no}` TTL 60s；任何写操作（建档/改档/基线确认/手术史/既往史/合并）后主动删除。8) 不返回手机号、证件号等隐私字段。 **错误码：** `"1103001"`、`40100`。

#### API-30 按患者取屈光档案（P-03 内部契约）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m11/records/by-patient/10021?include_closed=false
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "patient_id": 10021,
    "found": true,
    "record_no": "RG00001234",
    "patient_name": "张某某",
    "status": "ACTIVE",
    "record_level": "KEY",
    "is_high_myopia": true,
    "is_prior_refractive_surgery": true,
    "prior_surgery_count": 1,
    "latest_admission_result": "PASS",
    "latest_baseline_date": "2026-10-31",
    "last_optometry_no": "YG202610300008",
    "baseline_stale": false,
    "baseline": {
      "baseline_no": "JX202610310014",
      "baseline_version": 2,
      "exam_date": "2026-10-31",
      "surgery_feasibility": "NOT_FEASIBLE",
      "eyes": [
        { "eye_side": "OD", "sphere": -8.50, "cylinder": -1.25, "axis": 180,
          "cct_thinnest": 478.00, "axial_length": 26.18 },
        { "eye_side": "OS", "sphere": -8.00, "cylinder": -1.00, "axis": 10,
          "cct_thinnest": 496.00, "axial_length": 25.90 }
      ]
    },
    "surgery_histories": [
      { "surgery_seq": 1, "surgery_date": "2019-06-12", "refractive_type": "FS_LASIK",
        "eye_side": "OU", "outcome_result": "PARTIAL", "verified_flag": true }
    ],
    "contraindication_codes": ["DRY_EYE", "PRIOR_LASIK"],
    "state_version": 12,
    "updated_at": "2026-10-31T09:12:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2WY"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 内部服务鉴权（同 API-29）。2) 命中 `uk_m11_refractive_record_patient_active` 取在管档案（一患一档保证最多 1 条）；`include_closed = true` 时若无在管档案则返回最近一份已结档档案。3) 无档案时返回 `found = false`、`record_no = null`（**不报错**，便于 M07/M12/M16-04 直接用于新患者流程）。4) 返回内容与 API-29 一致但更精简（手术史只回摘要）；`baseline_stale` 同口径。5) 已合并患者：按 `merged_into_record_no` 链路追溯并返回最终在管档案（跟随一层跳转，最多 3 层）。6) 缓存：Redis `m11:record:patient:{patient_id}` TTL 60s；写操作后删除。7) 供 M07（医生工作站显示「已做屈光手术」标记）、M12（检查时提示既往手术）、M16-04（收费时校验术式合规）消费。 **错误码：** `40100`、`40001`（`patient_id` 非法）。

### 6.4 FastAPI 路由实现示例

```python
# app/api/v1/m11/record.py
"""M11-03 屈光档案 —— 对外 REST 路由层

路由前缀：/api/v1/m11/records
全部写接口支持 Idempotency-Key；读接口支持条件缓存。
"""

from datetime import date
from typing import Annotated, List, Literal, Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Header, Path, Query, status

from app.api.deps import CurrentUser, DbSession, require_perm
from app.schemas.m11.refractive_record import (
    BaselineConfirmIn,
    BaselineCreateIn,
    CorrectFieldIn,
    ExportRequestIn,
    MedicalHistoryIn,
    MergeApplyIn,
    MergeAuditIn,
    RecordCreateIn,
    RecordUpdateIn,
    SurgeryHistoryIn,
)
from app.services.m11.record_service import RecordService
from app.utils.response import ok

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/m11/records", tags=["M11-03 屈光档案"])


def get_record_service(db: DbSession) -> RecordService:
    """档案服务依赖。"""
    return RecordService(db)


RecordSvc = Annotated[RecordService, Depends(get_record_service)]


# ==================== 主页与选项 ====================


@router.get("/dashboard", summary="屈光档案主页汇总")
async def dashboard(
    svc: RecordSvc,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m11:record:view")),
    org_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0, description="院区 ID"),
):
    """返回卡片、待办、7 日趋势、级别与可行性分布、我最近处理的档案。"""
    data = await svc.dashboard(org_id=org_id, user_id=user.staff_id,
                               org_ids=user.org_ids)
    return ok(data, headers={"Cache-Control": "private, max-age=60"})


@router.get("/options", summary="下拉选项")
async def options(
    svc: RecordSvc,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m11:record:view")),
    scope: str = Query("status,level,doctor", description="逗号分隔的选项域"),
):
    """返回静态枚举、医师列表、停戴天数与阈值配置。"""
    return ok(await svc.options(scope=scope, org_ids=user.org_ids))


# ==================== 列表与详情 ====================


@router.get("", summary="档案分页列表与检索")
async def list_records(
    svc: RecordSvc,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m11:record:view")),
    keyword: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=32, description="档案号/姓名/电话"),
    status_: Optional[str] = Query(None, alias="status", description="状态多选"),
    record_level: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="级别多选"),
    manage_doctor_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, gt=0),
    date_from: Optional[date] = Query(None, description="建档日期起"),
    date_to: Optional[date] = Query(None, description="建档日期止"),
    is_high_myopia: Optional[bool] = Query(None, description="仅高度近视"),
    has_thin_cornea: Optional[bool] = Query(None, description="仅角膜偏薄"),
    stale_baseline: bool = Query(False, description="仅基线陈旧"),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
    sort: Literal["opened_at", "latest_baseline_date", "record_level",
                  "updated_at"] = Query("updated_at"),
    order: Literal["asc", "desc"] = Query("desc"),
):
    """按关键字、状态、级别、医师、日期与布尔标记分页查询档案。"""
    return ok(await svc.list_records(
        keyword=keyword, status=status_, record_level=record_level,
        manage_doctor_id=manage_doctor_id, date_from=date_from, date_to=date_to,
        is_high_myopia=is_high_myopia, has_thin_cornea=has_thin_cornea,
        stale_baseline=stale_baseline, page=page, page_size=page_size,
        sort=sort, order=order, org_ids=user.org_ids))


@router.get("/precheck", summary="建档前预检查")
async def precheck(
    svc: RecordSvc,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m11:record:view")),
    patient_id: int = Query(..., gt=0, description="患者 ID"),
):
    """检查患者是否存在、是否已有在管档案、是否有已合并档案链路。"""
    return ok(await svc.precheck(patient_id=patient_id))


@router.get("/export", summary="我的导出任务列表")
async def list_export_tasks(
    svc: RecordSvc,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m11:record:print")),
    task_status: str = Query("ALL", description="任务状态或 ALL"),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
):
    """返回当前用户发起的打印/导出任务。"""
    return ok(await svc.list_export_tasks(
        task_status=task_status, requester_id=user.staff_id,
        page=page, page_size=page_size))


@router.get("/export/{task_no}", summary="导出任务状态与下载地址")
async def get_export_task(
    svc: RecordSvc,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m11:record:print")),
    task_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32, description="任务号，如 DC202610310003"),
    with_checksum: bool = Query(False, description="是否实时校验文件 SHA-256"),
):
    """返回任务状态、带时效签名的下载地址与（可选）校验结果。"""
    return ok(await svc.get_export_task(
        task_no=task_no, requester_id=user.staff_id,
        with_checksum=with_checksum))


@router.get("/change-log", summary="变更审计查询")
async def query_change_log(
    svc: RecordSvc,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m11:record:audit_view")),
    record_no: Optional[str] = Query(None, max_length=32),
    object_type: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="对象类型"),
    action: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="动作"),
    sensitive_only: bool = Query(False, description="仅临床关键字段"),
    date_from: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    date_to: Optional[date] = Query(None),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
):
    """按档案、对象、动作与日期区间查询字段级审计（日期区间用于分区裁剪）。"""
    return ok(await svc.query_change_log(
        record_no=record_no, object_type=object_type, action=action,
        sensitive_only=sensitive_only, date_from=date_from, date_to=date_to,
        page=page, page_size=page_size, org_ids=user.org_ids))


@router.get("/{record_no}", summary="档案详情")
async def get_record(
    svc: RecordSvc,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m11:record:view")),
    record_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32, description="档案号，如 RG00001234"),
    include: str = Query("baseline,surgery,injury",
                         description="需要包含的段：baseline/surgery/injury/timeline_brief"),
):
    """返回档案主信息、当前生效基线（含双眼）、手术史、既往史与最近活动。"""
    return ok(await svc.get_detail(record_no=record_no, include=include))


# ==================== 建档与维护 ====================


@router.post("", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED, summary="建档")
async def create_record(
    svc: RecordSvc,
    payload: RecordCreateIn,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m11:record:create")),
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
):
    """一患一档建档，生成 RG 档案号并写入 RECORD_CREATED 时间轴事件。"""
    return ok(await svc.create(payload=payload, operator=user,
                               idempotency_key=idempotency_key))


@router.put("/{record_no}", summary="维护档案基本信息")
async def update_record(
    svc: RecordSvc,
    payload: RecordUpdateIn,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m11:record:update")),
    record_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
):
    """修改级别、主管医师、科室、标签与备注，使用乐观锁 version。"""
    return ok(await svc.update(record_no=record_no, payload=payload, operator=user))


@router.post("/{record_no}/close", summary="结档 / 重新激活")
async def close_record(
    svc: RecordSvc,
    payload: dict,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m11:record:update")),
    record_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
):
    """action=CLOSE 结档（需原因 ≥ 5 字）；action=REOPEN 重新激活。"""
    return ok(await svc.close_or_reopen(record_no=record_no, payload=payload,
                                        operator=user))


# ==================== 术前基线 ====================


@router.get("/{record_no}/baselines", summary="基线版本列表")
async def list_baselines(
    svc: RecordSvc,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m11:record:view")),
    record_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    include_void: bool = Query(False, description="是否包含已作废基线"),
):
    """返回该档案全部基线版本（按版本号倒序）。"""
    return ok(await svc.list_baselines(record_no=record_no, include_void=include_void))


@router.post("/{record_no}/baselines", status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
             summary="创建术前基线")
async def create_baseline(

    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m11:record:update")),

):
    """创建基线草稿（含双眼明细），服务端计算等效球镜、保守厚度与可行性。"""

                                        operator=user,
                                        idempotency_key=idempotency_key))


@router.get("/{record_no}/baselines/feasibility", summary="术式可行性初判")
async def calc_feasibility(
    svc: RecordSvc,
    user: CurrentUser = Depends(require_perm("m11:record:view")),
    record_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    cct_od: Optional[float] = Query(None, description="右眼中央厚度 μm"),
    cct_thinnest_od: Optional[float] = Query(None, description="右眼最薄点 μm"),
    cct_os: Optional[float] = Query(None, description="左眼中央厚度 μm"),
    cct_thinnest_os: Optional[float] = Query(None, description="左眼最薄点 μm"),
    topo_od: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="右眼地形图形态"),
    topo_os: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="左眼地形图形态"),
):
    """纯计算接口：按最薄点保守取值给出眼别与基线级可行性初判。"""
    return ok(await svc.calc_feasibility(
        record_no=record_no, cct_od=cct_od, cct_thinnest_od=cct_thinnest_od,
        cct_os=cct_os, cct_thinnest_os=cct_thinnest_os,
        topo_od=topo_od, topo_os=topo_os))


@router.put("/{record_no}/baselines/{baseline_no}", summary="修改基线草稿")

):

    return ok(await svc.confirm_baseline(record_no=record_no, baseline_no=baseline_no,

@router.post("/{record_no}/baselines/{baseline_no}/void", summary="作废基线")

                                      payload=payload, operator=user))

        include_offline=include_offline, cursor=cursor, limit=limit))
```
